###########SEPARATISM - VARIABLES###########
#Separatism Modifier
modify_separatism_support = {
	add_to_variable = { separatism = modify_separatism }
	custom_effect_tooltip = GENERIC_separatism_tt
	clamp_variable = {
		var = separatism
		min = 1
		max = 100
	}
}
#Separatism TAT
GENERIC_update_separatism_rebellion_TAT = {
	#Check TAT
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { TAT.separatism > 75 }
			TAT = {
				NOT = { 
					has_country_flag = GENERIC_separatism_rebeliion_flag
				}
			}
			NOT = { 
				country_exists = TAT
			}
		}
		661 = { owner = { country_event = separatism.1 } }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { TAT.separatism > 35 }
			check_variable = { TAT.separatism < 51 }
			TAT = {
				NOT = { 
					has_country_flag = GENERIC_separatism_rebeliion_flag
				}
			}
			NOT = { 
				country_exists = TAT
			}
		}
		661 = {
			add_dynamic_modifier = {
				modifier = separatists_activity
				days = 32
			}
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { TAT.separatism > 50 }
			TAT = {
				NOT = { 
					has_country_flag = GENERIC_separatism_rebeliion_flag
				}
			}
			NOT = { 
				country_exists = TAT
			}
		}
		661 = {
			owner = {
				if = {
					limit = { 661 = { is_core_of = PREV } }
				}
				remove_state_core = 661
			}
			start_resistance = yes
			set_resistance = 50
		}
	}
}

	if = {
		limit = {
			owns_state = 1079
			OR = {
				has_idea = SOV_fsb_idea
				has_idea = SOV_fsb_idea2
				has_idea = SOV_fsb_idea3
			}
			NOT = {
				country_exists = GDR
			}
		}
		GDR = {
			set_temp_variable = { modify_separatism = -6 }
			modify_separatism_support = yes
		}
	}
	#Separatism Down - GDR 2
	if = {
		limit = {
			owns_state = 539
			OR = {
				has_idea = SOV_fsb_idea
				has_idea = SOV_fsb_idea2
				has_idea = SOV_fsb_idea3
			}
			NOT = {
				country_exists = GDR
			}
		}
		GDR = {
			set_temp_variable = { modify_separatism = -6 }
			modify_separatism_support = yes
		}
	}
	#Separatism Down - GDR 3
	if = {
		limit = {
			owns_state = 44
			OR = {
				has_idea = SOV_fsb_idea
				has_idea = SOV_fsb_idea2
				has_idea = SOV_fsb_idea3
			}
			NOT = {
				country_exists = GDR
			}
		}
		GDR = {
			set_temp_variable = { modify_separatism = -6 }
			modify_separatism_support = yes
		}
	}
	#Separatism Down - RHE
	if = {
		limit = {
			owns_state = 40
			OR = {
				has_idea = SOV_fsb_idea
				has_idea = SOV_fsb_idea2
				has_idea = SOV_fsb_idea3
			}
			NOT = {
				country_exists = RHE
			}
		}
		RHE = {
			set_temp_variable = { modify_separatism = -6 }
			modify_separatism_support = yes
		}
	}
}
